## 🤖🔧 Assistant
<details>
<summary>Edit DEVELOPMENT_PLAN.md</summary>

**Before:**
```markdown
The `BSplineHypothesis` owns the fitted `Geom_BSplineSurface` (stage 3 reuses the same object via fresh handles) plus a cached, seed-rechecking `ShapeAnalysis_Surface` projector used by absorption, refinement, `--compare`, and stage-3 charts/normals/projections. Stage-3 charts of B-spline faces also carry a parameter-space `home` anchor (the projection of the region's mean face centroid): a projection landing on a coincident-boundary curve of a seam-split band is ambiguous between the two parameter extremes (Newton, the gap re-check, and `ValueOfUV` all cannot tell — both branches are exact), and the branch nearer the face's own interior wins. The stage 3.1 compare **exempts** edges whose two faces share a `shared_surface_group` (and the corner vertices such edge pairs meet at): they are reconstruction artifacts flagged at fit time, with no counterpart in a reference model, which represents the band as one periodic face with a seam. The exemption is tag-based, never tolerance-based — the edge compare is precisely the check that catches over-segmentation, and it stays fully armed for every edge the reconstruction did not deliberately manufacture.

**Results (2026-07-10)**: all 8 `tests/nurbs/` models pass full `--compare` (volume within 2.7e-6 … 5.2e-3 relative); `elliptical_cylinder` (closed freeform wall) passes via the seam-split reconstruction. Full 477-test suite green.
```

**After:**
```markdown
The `BSplineHypothesis` owns the fitted `Geom_BSplineSurface` (stage 3 reuses the same object via fresh handles) plus a cached, seed-rechecking `ShapeAnalysis_Surface` projector used by absorption, refinement, `--compare`, and stage-3 charts/normals/projections. Stage-3 charts of B-spline faces also carry a parameter-space `home` anchor (the projection of the region's mean face centroid): a projection landing on a coincident-boundary curve of a seam-split band (the fallback) is ambiguous between the two parameter extremes (Newton, the gap re-check, and `ValueOfUV` all cannot tell — both branches are exact), and the branch nearer the face's own interior wins. On a periodic (u_periodic) chart the U-arm of that resolution is replaced by principal-value reduction to [0, 2π) — the same contract as the analytic periodic charts — and the whole wrapped-face machinery (edge-sample branch unwrap, winding classification, seam planning/anchoring, pcurve shifts) applies verbatim. There is no compare exemption: the stage 3.1 edge/vertex compare is fully armed for every edge, including the seam (a real feature both the reconstruction and the reference have).

**Periodic-band junctions (stage 3.3)**: the band's two cap junctions are closed-loop edges (no vertices) and must carry PERIODIC curves — seam planning re-anchors a closed edge with `MakeEdge` over [t, t+period] at a solved seam azimuth, which a closed-but-non-periodic curve refuses. Two mechanisms deliver that (both were needed on elliptical_cylinder): (a) an IntSS curve that comes back closed-but-non-periodic (observed type: `Geom_TrimmedCurve`, exact to ~1e-14) is converted via `GeomConvert::CurveToBSplineCurve` + `SetPeriodic` (exact, measured); (b) an IntSS curve that comes back as a genuine partial arc falls to the boundary-chain repair, whose closed-chain arm interpolates a periodic C2 B-spline through the chain vertices (`GeomAPI_Interpolate` with the periodic flag, closing point not repeated — ~4e-15 chain deviation observed). Partial-arc detection for closed loops keys on the ENDPOINT GAP, not the parameter span: a closed B-spline junction arrives over an arbitrary range (observed [0,1]), and the old span≠2π test replaced the true junction with a bogus circle (14.8 mm off, 12% volume error).

**Results (2026-07-15)**: all 8 `tests/nurbs/` models pass full `--compare`; `elliptical_cylinder` (closed freeform wall) reconstructs as ONE periodic B-spline face with a real seam edge, volume within 8.6e-5 relative (was 5.2e-3 under the seam-split), with no compare exemption anywhere. Full 477-test suite green.
```

435	- *Keep the seam-split as fallback* for closed bands the periodic path cannot handle.

439	- *The around-the-band axis is NOT always U.* Which square axis wraps depends on which sides the corner chooser assigns to the cut copies: for elliptical_cylinder the cut copies landed as the 2-vertex u-sides, so **v** is the around axis (93-vertex ring sides). \`split_cut_hypothesis\` already detects this dynamically (the axis whose parameter extremes carry \`CUT_OUTSIDE\` loop entries); the periodic pipeline needs the same detection, must feed \`Interpolate\` with the around axis as the FIRST array index (transpose when around = v), and must rescale/periodize the matching knot vector (\`SetUKnots\`+\`SetUPeriodic\` vs the V variants). 447	
448	**Implementation notes for the NURBS pass** (knowledge from the stage-3 rework, 2026-07-08; all items below are now implemented as described — kept for reference):
449	
450	- *The chart contract.* A chart provides \`point_uv(p) → (u, v, pole_radius)\` and \`point_at(u, v)\`, plus \`u_periodic()\`/\`v_periodic()\`. For a B-spline chart: \`pole_radius\` is ∞ (like the plane chart — fitted height-field patches have no UV degeneracies), and **both periodic flags must be false**. Every mod-2π unwrap/branch-lift in stage 3.4 is gated on those flags; reducing non-periodic parameter coordinates mod 2π silently corrupts them (this was a real bug on plane charts, masked for months by adaptive densification — see LEARNINGS). With both flags false, the whole seam/winding/degenerate-pole machinery self-disables and B-spline faces take the plain-loop path end to end.
451	- *The chart must wrap the exact \`Handle(Geom_BSplineSurface)\` stored in \`FaceDescriptor.surface\`* — never a reconstruction. (Analytic charts get away with rebuilding the gp_Ax3 only because that construction is deterministic.) Use one \`ShapeAnalysis_Surface\` per face: \`new_handlegeomsurface\` + \`value_of_uv(&mut self, pnt, precision)\` — note \`&mut\` (it caches internally). For sampling along an edge curve, prefer \`next_value_of_uv(prev_uv, pnt, preci, maxpreci)\` — it seeds the search from the previous sample, the B-spline analog of the analytic charts' branch-continuity unwrap. Validate each solution with \`gap()\` (ValueOfUV can return a poor projection rather than failing) and fall back to a fresh \`value_of_uv\` when the gap exceeds tolerance.
452	- *Every \`match\` on \`SelectedSurface\` needs a \`BSpline\` arm.* From crawling all of them: \`create_occt_surface\` and \`surface_faces\` (3.1), \`surface_normal_at_point\` (3.2 tangency detection and the 3.4 orientation compare — use D1 cross product on the handle), \`project_point_to_selected\` (corner-vertex refinement), \`build_face_chart\`, the concave-flag matches in \`assemble_face\`/\`create_occt_faces_all\`, the tangent-edge dispatch in 3.3, and the cosmetic \`stype\` labels + viz overlays.

Transposition negates d1u×d1v, so the outward-orientation flip reverses the second index and XORs with the transpose.

- *Junction curves*: see "Periodic-band junctions (stage 3.3)" above — closed-loop edges need periodic curves for seam re-anchoring; both the GeomConvert periodicization and the periodic chain-repair arm were required on the first model.

- **§2.7 design rules (each earned by a measured model failure — don't undo):** (1) 2.7 runs BEFORE absorb (absorb hides accidental fits from gathering — saddle). (2) Gathering absorbs ≤16-face selections across ≤1× angular-tolerance edges; guards: ≥1 single-face planar member, absorbed ≤ singles. (3) Tangent-plane merge when a multi-face plane's ENTIRE component boundary is smooth (blister). (4) B-splines never absorb/refine TARGETS (OCCT surfaces extrapolate — arch 1.7%). (5) Grid stations AT boundary-vertex pinned positions subdivided to ~1.5·√n interior target (mid-chord stations pin to the chordal prism; vertex-u quantiles → singular interpolation → f64::MAX garbage).
- **OCCT gotchas:** `PointsToBSplineSurface` approx reports IsDone while overshooting Tol3D by mm (interpolation trustworthy, approximation not). `NextValueOfUV` Newton settles in nearby local minima; `ValueOfUV` global search too coarse on many-knot surfaces — every projection needs a gap re-check (PROJ_GAP_RECHECK=0.02mm) and validation seeds from each vertex's OWN Floater UV. BRepGProp quadrature untrustworthy on freeform faces — gen-test-models validates against mesh volume.

Env probes: BREPPER_DEBUG_EDGE_UV, BREPPER_DEBUG_SPLIT; `-vvv` fit-residual-vs-own-grid probe separates fit vs sampler/projection failure.
- **--compare is strict**: >1% volume and unreadable STEP are hard errors; `-o /dev/null` incompatible (run_test_table.sh writes a temp file). <!-- msg_011Cd4kwdLF3DFEJYPyC8tsT -->

Done — TODO item #1 is complete, committed, and the acceptance test (deleting the compare exemption) is delivered. Closed freeform bands now reconstruct as **one U-periodic B-spline face with a real seam edge** — the form CAD kernels write — instead of the seam-split's two faces with exempted artificial edges.

**How it works** (commit `a61f408`, after a small docs commit `a2bfc8a` for the earlier TODO→DONE move):

- **Stage 2.7** fits closed bands with `Interpolate(thePeriodic=true)`: the around-the-band axis goes in as the array's first index (transposed when that axis is v in the Floater square), with the closing station omitted — a probe (`tmp/probe_periodic.rs`, confirmed against the OCCT C++ source) established that OCCT appends the wrap-around row itself. The fitted knots are then rescaled to exactly [0, 2π] via `SetUKnots` (exact, ~1e-15 drift), which lets stage 3.4's existing 2π-hard-coded wrapped-face machinery — branch unwrap, winding, seam planning, pcurves — apply verbatim.
- **Stage 3**'s B-spline chart now reports `u_periodic()` and returns principal U like the analytic charts. The seam gauge came free: chord-length parameters start at the first supplied row, so the U=0 iso *is* the cut path, and `plan_seam_anchors`' vertex-pair search (which would have failed on the azimuthally-misaligned strip rings) is never consulted.
- Two junction fixes were both required, exactly as the plan predicted: closed-loop edges must carry *periodic* curves for seam re-anchoring, so closed-but-non-periodic IntSS curves get converted (`GeomConvert` + `SetPeriodic`), and a genuinely partial IntSS arc falls to a new **periodic chain-repair arm** (`GeomAPI_Interpolate`, 4e-15 deviation) — which incidentally completed the *other* deferred NURBS TODO item too. Also fixed en route: partial-arc detection keyed on parameter span, which was replacing the elliptical junction with a bogus circle (12% volume error).

**Results:** elliptical_cylinder passes full `--compare` at **8.6e-5 relative volume** (was 5.2e-3 under the seam-split — 60× better), with the stage 3.1 exemption, `shared_surface_group`, and `is_seam_cut_pair` deleted so the edge compare is fully armed everywhere. All 477 tests green, clippy clean. The clamped seam-split survives only as an untagged fallback, currently exercised by no model.

[TODO.md](TODO.md)'s NURBS follow-ups section is now empty — remaining deferred items are just the seeding-mask verification, OBJ/PLY input, and IGES export. [DEVELOPMENT_PLAN.md](DEVELOPMENT_PLAN.md) §2.7 now documents the as-built periodic design, and [DONE.md](DONE.md) records the completion.
